Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in geometry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 527 and the invariant I(A,B) = 60 mod 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_527(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 527. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^60(S, F) is trivial. QED.
